{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/bin/bash\\n# ============================================================================\\n# run_full_m2_training.sh — 全量 M2 单次训练 wrapper\\n#\\n# Usage:\\n#   bash run_full_m2_training.sh &lt;strategy> &lt;seed> &lt;gpu_id>\\n#\\n# Args:\\n#   strategy  — hard-negative 策略: random | embedding_distance | dataflow_aware\\n#   seed      — 随机种子: 42 | 43 | 44\\n#   gpu_id    — GPU 编号: 0 | 1\\n#\\n# Example:\\n#   bash run_full_m2_training.sh random 42 0\\n#   bash run_full_m2_training.sh embedding_distance 43 1\\n#\\n# 训练参数 (D013 确认):\\n#   batch_size=8, num_negatives=7, max_seq_length=2048, epochs=2\\n#   eval_steps=500, lr=2e-5, LoRA (r=16), gradient checkpointing\\n#\\n# 输出:\\n#   /root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/full_m2_outputs/${strategy}_s${seed}/\\n#\\n# 注意:\\n#   - 使用 --early-stopping-patience 3 (D013 配置)\\n# ============================================================================\\n\\nset -euo pipefail\\n\\n# ── 参数解析 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\nSTRATEGY=\\\"${1:?Usage: $0 &lt;strategy> &lt;seed> &lt;gpu_id>}\\\"\\nSEED=\\\"${2:?Usage: $0 &lt;strategy> &lt;seed> &lt;gpu_id>}\\\"\\nGPU_ID=\\\"${3:?Usage: $0 &lt;strategy> &lt;seed> &lt;gpu_id>}\\\"\\n\\nVALID_STRATEGIES=\\\"random embedding_distance dataflow_aware\\\"\\nif ! echo \\\"$VALID_STRATEGIES\\\" | grep -qw \\\"$STRATEGY\\\"; then\\n    echo \\\"ERROR: strategy must be one of: $VALID_STRATEGIES\\\"\\n    exit 1\\nfi\\n\\n# ── 路径 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\nPROJECT_ROOT=\\\"/root/structfuse\\\"\\nMODEL_PATH=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/models/Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-3B\\\"\\nDATA_DIR=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/full_m2_training_data\\\"\\nOUTPUT_DIR=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/full_m2_outputs/${STRATEGY}_s${SEED}\\\"\\nTRAIN_FILE=\\\"${DATA_DIR}/train_${STRATEGY}_s${SEED}.jsonl\\\"\\nVAL_FILE=\\\"${DATA_DIR}/val_${STRATEGY}_s${SEED}.jsonl\\\"\\nDATAFLOW_GRAPH=\\\"${DATA_DIR}/dataflow_graph.json\\\"\\nEMBEDDINGS_FILE=\\\"${DATA_DIR}/embeddings.npz\\\"\\nLOG_FILE=\\\"${OUTPUT_DIR}/train.log\\\"\\n\\n# ── 环境变量 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\nexport CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=\\\"$GPU_ID\\\"\\nexport WANDB_MODE=offline\\nexport TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1\\nexport HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1\\nexport PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1\\n\\n# ── 前置检查 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\necho \\\"==========================================\\\"\\necho \\\" M2 Full Training\\\"\\necho \\\" strategy=$STRATEGY  seed=$SEED  gpu=$GPU_ID\\\"\\necho \\\" output=$OUTPUT_DIR\\\"\\necho \\\"==========================================\\\"\\n\\nfor f in \\\"$TRAIN_FILE\\\" \\\"$VAL_FILE\\\" \\\"$MODEL_PATH/config.json\\\"; do\\n    if [ ! -e \\\"$f\\\" ]; then\\n        echo \\\"ERROR: required file not found: $f\\\"\\n        exit 1\\n    fi\\ndone\\n\\nmkdir -p \\\"$OUTPUT_DIR\\\"\\n\\n# ── 清理函数 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\ncleanup() {\\n    EXIT_CODE=$?\\n    if [ $EXIT_CODE -ne 0 ]; then\\n        echo \\\"[$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')] FAILED: strategy=$STRATEGY seed=$SEED gpu=$GPU_ID exit_code=$EXIT_CODE\\\" | tee -a \\\"$LOG_FILE\\\"\\n    else\\n        echo \\\"[$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')] DONE: strategy=$STRATEGY seed=$SEED gpu=$GPU_ID\\\" | tee -a \\\"$LOG_FILE\\\"\\n    fi\\n}\\ntrap cleanup EXIT\\n\\n# ── 构建训练命令 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\nTRAIN_CMD=(\\n    python -u \\\"${PROJECT_ROOT}/training/train.py\\\"\\n    --model-name \\\"$MODEL_PATH\\\"\\n    --train-file \\\"$TRAIN_FILE\\\"\\n    --val-file \\\"$VAL_FILE\\\"\\n    --output-dir \\\"$OUTPUT_DIR\\\"\\n    --hard-negative-strategy \\\"$STRATEGY\\\"\\n    --seed \\\"$SEED\\\"\\n    --batch-size 8\\n    --num-negatives 7\\n    --max-seq-length 2048\\n    --num-epochs 2\\n    --learning-rate 2e-5\\n    --eval-steps 500\\n    --save-steps 1000\\n    --use-lora\\n    --gradient-checkpointing\\n    --wandb-project structfuse\\n    --wandb-run-name \\\"full_m2_${STRATEGY}_s${SEED}\\\"\\n    --early-stopping-patience 3\\n)\\n\\n# dataflow_aware 和 embedding_distance 需要额外文件\\nif [ \\\"$STRATEGY\\\" = \\\"dataflow_aware\\\" ] && [ -f \\\"$DATAFLOW_GRAPH\\\" ]; then\\n    TRAIN_CMD+=(--dataflow-graph-file \\\"$DATAFLOW_GRAPH\\\")\\nfi\\nif [ \\\"$STRATEGY\\\" = \\\"embedding_distance\\\" ] && [ -f \\\"$EMBEDDINGS_FILE\\\" ]; then\\n    TRAIN_CMD+=(--embeddings-file \\\"$EMBEDDINGS_FILE\\\")\\nfi\\n\\n# ── 执行 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\necho \\\"[$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')] START: ${TRAIN_CMD[*]}\\\" | tee \\\"$LOG_FILE\\\"\\n\\\"${TRAIN_CMD[@]}\\\" 2>&1 | tee -a \\\"$LOG_FILE\\\"\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"}
```

Key finding: the `--hard-negative-strategy` parameter only accepts `random`, `embedding_distance`, `dataflow_aware` — **not** `bm25`. I need to check if there's a way to handle BM25 data. 

Looking at the data files, the BM25 data files are pre-mined (`train_bm25_s42.jsonl`), so the negatives are already embedded in the data. The `--hard-negative-strategy` flag might be just for dynamic mining during training, while the pre-mined data already has the negatives baked in.
 
So the training script uses the strategy parameter to control which miner runs during dynamic mining, but the pre-generated data files already contain negatives selected offline — meaning I can use the BM25 data directly without needing to pass it as a strategy flag.

The issue is that the `choices` constraint on line 586 won't accept "bm25" as a valid strategy option. I need to either add it to the allowed choices in train.py or work around it by passing a different strategy since the negatives are already embedded in the data files themselves.

Let me trace through how the strategy parameter actually gets used in the training code to figure out the best approach.

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Data-flow-aware hard negative mining for StructFuse.\\n\\nThree strategies:\\n  1. random         — baseline, random non-positive from same repo\\n  2. embedding_distance — CoCoHaNeRe-style nearest-neighbour non-positives\\n  3. dataflow_aware — lexically similar but data-flow disconnected (our contribution)\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport random as stdlib_random\\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Set, Tuple\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\n\\n\\nclass HardNegativeMiner(ABC):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Base class for hard-negative mining strategies.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(\\n        self,\\n        dataflow_graph: Dict[str, Set[str]] | None = None,\\n        embeddings: Dict[str, np.ndarray] | None = None,\\n        bm25_index: object | None = None,\\n    ):\\n        self.dataflow_graph = dataflow_graph or {}\\n        self.embeddings = embeddings or {}\\n        self.bm25_index = bm25_index\\n\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def mine(\\n        self,\\n        query: str,\\n        positives: List[str],\\n        candidates: List[str],\\n        num_negatives: int,\\n    ) -> List[Tuple[str, float]]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Return list of (candidate_id, hardness_score) tuples.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        ...\\n\\n    # ── shared helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\n    @staticmethod\\n    def _exclude(candidates: List[str], exclude: Set[str]) -> List[str]:\\n        return [c for c in candidates if c not in exclude]\\n\\n    @staticmethod\\n    def load_embeddings(path: str | Path) -> Dict[str, np.ndarray]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Load pre-computed embeddings from a .npz file (keys=file ids).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        data = np.load(path, allow_pickle=True)\\n        return {str(k): data[k] for k in data.files}\\n\\n    @staticmethod\\n    def save_embeddings(embs: Dict[str, np.ndarray], path: str | Path) -> None:\\n        np.savez(path, **embs)\\n\\n\\nclass RandomMiner(HardNegativeMiner):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Uniformly random negatives from same-repo non-positives.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def mine(self, query, positives, candidates, num_negatives):\\n        pool = self._exclude(candidates, {query, *positives})\\n        sampled = stdlib_random.sample(pool, min(num_negatives, len(pool)))\\n        return [(s, 0.0) for s in sampled]\\n\\n\\nclass EmbeddingDistanceMiner(HardNegativeMiner):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Nearest-neighbour negatives by cosine similarity (CoCoHaNeRe baseline).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, **kwargs):\\n        super().__init__(**kwargs)\\n        self._matrix: np.ndarray | None = None\\n        self._keys: List[str] | None = None\\n\\n    def _build_matrix(self):\\n        if self._matrix is not None:\\n            return\\n        self._keys = sorted(self.embeddings.keys())\\n        vecs = np.stack([self.embeddings[k] for k in self._keys]).astype(np.float32)\\n        norms = np.linalg.norm(vecs, axis=1, keepdims=True).clip(min=1e-8)\\n        self._matrix = vecs / norms\\n\\n    def mine(self, query, positives, candidates, num_negatives):\\n        self._build_matrix()\\n        if query not in self.embeddings:\\n            return RandomMiner().mine(query, positives, candidates, num_negatives)\\n\\n        exclude = {query, *positives}\\n        q_vec = self.embeddings[query].astype(np.float32)\\n        q_vec = q_vec / max(np.linalg.norm(q_vec), 1e-8)\\n\\n        # cosine similarity against all candidates\\n        cand_set = set(self._exclude(candidates, exclude))\\n        indices = [i for i, k in enumerate(self._keys) if k in cand_set]\\n        if not indices:\\n            return []\\n\\n        sims = self._matrix[indices] @ q_vec  # (N_cand,)\\n        top_k = min(num_negatives, len(indices))\\n        top_idx = np.argpartition(-sims, top_k)[:top_k]\\n        top_idx = top_idx[np.argsort(-sims[top_idx])]\\n\\n        return [(self._keys[indices[i]], float(sims[i])) for i in top_idx]\\n\\n\\nclass DataFlowAwareMiner(HardNegativeMiner):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Select lexically/embedding-similar but data-flow-disconnected files.\\n\\n    Core insight: a file that *looks* related (high BM25 or embedding sim) but\\n    has no data-flow path to the query provides the strongest contrastive signal.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, top_k_similar: int = 50, **kwargs):\\n        super().__init__(**kwargs)\\n        self.top_k_similar = top_k_similar\\n        self._emb_miner = EmbeddingDistanceMiner(\\n            embeddings=self.embeddings,\\n            dataflow_graph=self.dataflow_graph,\\n        )\\n\\n    def _is_reachable(self, src: str, dst: str) -> bool:\\n        return dst in self.dataflow_graph.get(src, set())\\n\\n    def mine(self, query, positives, candidates, num_negatives):\\n        exclude = {query, *positives}\\n\\n        # Step 1: get top-K similar candidates (by embedding distance)\\n        similar = self._emb_miner.mine(\\n            query, positives, candidates, self.top_k_similar\\n        )\\n\\n        # Step 2: filter to those NOT reachable via data-flow\\n        disconnected = [\\n            (fid, score)\\n            for fid, score in similar\\n            if fid not in exclude and not self._is_reachable(query, fid)\\n        ]\\n\\n        # Step 3: if BM25 index available, re-rank by BM25 similarity\\n        if self.bm25_index is not None and hasattr(self.bm25_index, \\\"get_scores\\\"):\\n            disconnected = self._rerank_bm25(query, disconnected)\\n\\n        selected = disconnected[:num_negatives]\\n\\n        # fallback: pad with random if not enough disconnected candidates\\n        if len(selected) < num_negatives:\\n            used = {fid for fid, _ in selected} | exclude\\n            pool = [c for c in candidates if c not in used]\\n            extra = stdlib_random.sample(pool, min(num_negatives - len(selected), len(pool)))\\n            selected.extend([(e, 0.0) for e in extra])\\n\\n        return selected\\n\\n    def _rerank_bm25(\\n        self, query: str, candidates: List[Tuple[str, float]]\\n    ) -> List[Tuple[str, float]]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Re-rank disconnected candidates by BM25 score (descending).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        from rank_bm25 import BM25Okapi\\n\\n        if not isinstance(self.bm25_index, BM25Okapi):\\n            return candidates\\n\\n        # BM25 scores expect a tokenized query — use simple whitespace split\\n        scores = self.bm25_index.get_scores(query.split())\\n        id_to_bm25: Dict[str, float] = {}\\n        if hasattr(self.bm25_index, \\\"_doc_ids\\\"):\\n            for idx, doc_id in enumerate(self.bm25_index._doc_ids):\\n                id_to_bm25[doc_id] = float(scores[idx])\\n\\n        reranked = sorted(\\n            candidates,\\n            key=lambda t: id_to_bm25.get(t[0], 0.0),\\n            reverse=True,\\n        )\\n        return reranked\\n\\n\\n# ── batch mining helper ─────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef mine_batch(\\n    miner: HardNegativeMiner,\\n    queries: List[str],\\n    positives_map: Dict[str, List[str]],\\n    all_candidates: List[str],\\n    num_negatives: int,\\n) -> Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Mine negatives for a batch of queries. Suitable for epoch-level pre-mining.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    results = {}\\n    for q in queries:\\n        pos = positives_map.get(q, [])\\n        results[q] = miner.mine(q, pos, all_candidates, num_negatives)\\n    return results\\n\\n\\ndef build_miner(\\n    strategy: str,\\n    dataflow_graph: Dict[str, Set[str]] | None = None,\\n    embeddings: Dict[str, np.ndarray] | None = None,\\n    bm25_index: object | None = None,\\n    **kwargs,\\n) -> HardNegativeMiner:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Factory for miner instantiation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    cls_map = {\\n        \\\"random\\\": RandomMiner,\\n        \\\"embedding_distance\\\": EmbeddingDistanceMiner,\\n        \\\"dataflow_aware\\\": DataFlowAwareMiner,\\n    }\\n    if strategy not in cls_map:\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\"Unknown strategy '{strategy}', choose from {list(cls_map)}\\\")\\n    return cls_map[strategy](\\n        dataflow_graph=dataflow_graph,\\n        embeddings=embeddings,\\n        bm25_index=bm25_index,\\n        **kwargs,\\n    )\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"}

     for fid, score in similar\\n            if fid not in exclude and not self._is_reachable(query, fid)\\n        ]\\n\\n        # Step 3: if BM25 index available, re-rank by BM25 similarity\\n        if self.bm25_index is not None and hasattr(self.bm25_index, \\\"get_scores\\\"):\\n            disconnected = self._rerank_bm25(query, disconnected)\\n\\n        selected = disconnected[:num_negatives]\\n\\n        # fallback: pad with random if not enough disconnected candidates\\n        if len(selected) < num_negatives:\\n            used = {fid for fid, _ in selected} | exclude\\n            pool = [c for c in candidates if c not in used]\\n            extra = stdlib_random.sample(pool, min(num_negatives - len(selected), len(pool)))\\n            selected.extend([(e, 0.0) for e in extra])\\n\\n        return selected\\n\\n    def _rerank_bm25(\\n        self, query: str, candidates: List[Tuple[str, float]]\\n    ) -> List[Tuple[str, float]]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Re-rank disconnected candidates by BM25 score (descending).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        from rank_bm25 import BM25Okapi\\n\\n        if not isinstance(self.bm25_index, BM25Okapi):\\n            return candidates\\n\\n        # BM25 scores expect a tokenized query — use simple whitespace split\\n        scores = self.bm25_index.get_scores(query.split())\\n        id_to_bm25: Dict[str, float] = {}\\n        if hasattr(self.bm25_index, \\\"_doc_ids\\\"):\\n            for idx, doc_id in enumerate(self.bm25_index._doc_ids):\\n                id_to_bm25[doc_id] = float(scores[idx])\\n\\n        reranked = sorted(\\n            candidates,\\n            key=lambda t: id_to_bm25.get(t[0], 0.0),\\n            reverse=True,\\n        )\\n        return reranked\\n\\n\\n# ── batch mining helper ─────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef mine_batch(\\n    miner: HardNegativeMiner,\\n    queries: List[str],\\n    positives_map: Dict[str, List[str]],\\n    all_candidates: List[str],\\n    num_negatives: int,\\n) -> Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, float]]]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Mine negatives for a batch of queries. Suitable for epoch-level pre-mining.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    results = {}\\n    for q in queries:\\n        pos = positives_map.get(q, [])\\n        results[q] = miner.mine(q, pos, all_candidates, num_negatives)\\n    return results\\n\\n\\ndef build_miner(\\n    strategy: str,\\n    dataflow_graph: Dict[str, Set[str]] | None = None,\\n    embeddings: Dict[str, np.ndarray] | None = None,\\n    bm25_index: object | None = None,\\n    **kwargs,\\n) -> HardNegativeMiner:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Factory for miner instantiation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    cls_map = {\\n        \\\"random\\\": RandomMiner,\\n        \\\"embedding_distance\\\": EmbeddingDistanceMiner,\\n        \\\"dataflow_aware\\\": DataFlowAwareMiner,\\n    }\\n    if strategy not in cls_map:\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\"Unknown strategy '{strategy}', choose from {list(cls_map)}\\\")\\n    return cls_map[strategy](\\n        dataflow_graph=dataflow_graph,\\n        embeddings=embeddings,\\n        bm25_index=bm25_index,\\n        **kwargs,\\n    )\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I understand the full picture:

1. `train.py` line 586: `choices=["random", "embedding_distance", "dataflow_aware"]` — no "bm25"
2. `hard_negative_mining.py` `build_miner()`: `cls_map` only has random, embedding_distance, dataflow_aware — no bm25
3. The strategy is used in `build_miner` (line 326-327 of train.py) which creates a miner
4. If `--dynamic-mining` is NOT set, the miner is still created but only used for initial mining (non-dynamic path at line 387)


5. The miner gets instantiated regardless of the `--dynamic-mining` flag, so if I pass "bm25" as the strategy, it'll fail when `build_miner` tries to look it up in `cls_map` since it's not registered there.

I need to add "bm25" to both the command-line choices in `train.py` and register it in the `cls_map` dictionary in `hard_negative_mining.py`.

Since the miner isn't actually used when dynamic mining is disabled (the pre-mined negatives from the data files are used instead), I can safely map "bm25" to `RandomMiner` as a fallback — it'll satisfy the initialization requirement without affecting the actual training behavior.
```

  "command": "cat > /root/structfuse/scripts/run_m2_full_bm25_qwen.sh << 'SCRIPT_EOF'\n#!/bin/bash\n# ============================================================================\n# run_m2_full_bm25_qwen.sh — BM25 negative strategy Phase 2 training (Qwen)\n#\n# Runs 3 seeds sequentially. Config matches other M2 strategies exactly,\n# except: s42 uses 3 epochs; s43/s44 use 2 epochs + early_stopping_patience=2.\n#\n# Usage:\n#   nohup bash run_m2_full_bm25_qwen.sh > /root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/full_m2_outputs/bm25_qwen_schedule.log 2>&1 &\n# ============================================================================\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nPROJECT_ROOT=\"/root/structfuse\"\nMODEL_PATH=\"/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/models/Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-3B\"\nDATA_DIR=\"/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/full_m2_training_data\"\nOUTPUT_BASE=\"/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/full_m2_outputs\"\nGPU_ID=\"${1:-0}\"\n\nexport CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=\"$GPU_ID\"\nexport WANDB_MODE=offline\nexport TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1\nexport HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1\nexport PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1\n\n# Activate conda\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\n\n# ── Seed configs: (seed, epochs, early_stopping_patience) ──\ndeclare -a SEEDS=(42 43 44)\ndeclare -A EPOCHS=([42]=3 [43]=2 [44]=2)\ndeclare -A PATIENCE=([42]=0 [43]=2 [44]=2)\n\necho \"============================================\"\necho \" BM25 Qwen M2 Full Training\"\necho \" Seeds: ${SEEDS[*]}\"\necho \" GPU: $GPU_ID\"\necho \" Start: $(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')\"\necho \"============================================\"\n\nfor SEED in \"${SEEDS[@]}\"; do\n    STRATEGY=\"bm25\"\n    NUM_EPOCHS=\"${EPOCHS[$SEED]}\"\n    ESP=\"${PATIENCE[$SEED]}\"\n    OUTPUT_DIR=\"${OUTPUT_BASE}/bm25_s${SEED}\"\n    TRAIN_FILE=\"${DATA_DIR}/train_bm25_s${SEED}.jsonl\"\n    VAL_FILE=\"${DATA_DIR}/val_bm25_s${SEED}.jsonl\"\n    LOG_FILE=\"${OUTPUT_DIR}/train.log\"\n\n    # Pre-flight checks\n    for f in \"$TRAIN_FILE\" \"$VAL_FILE\" \"$MODEL_PATH/config.json\"; do\n        if [ ! -e \"$f\" ]; then\n            echo \"ERROR: required file not found: $f\"\n            exit 1\n        fi\n    done\n\n    mkdir -p \"$OUTPUT_DIR\"\n\n    echo \"\"\n    echo \"[$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')] START bm25_s${SEED} (epochs=${NUM_EPOCHS}, patience=${ESP})\"\n\n    TRAIN_CMD=(\n        python -u \"${PROJECT_ROOT}/training/train.py\"\n        --model-name \"$MODEL_PATH\"\n        --train-file \"$TRAIN_FILE\"\n        --val-file \"$VAL_FILE\"\n        --output-dir \"$OUTPUT_DIR\"\n        --hard-negative-strategy \"$STRATEGY\"\n        --seed \"$SEED\"\n        --batch-size 8\n        --num-negatives 7\n        --max-seq-length 2048\n        --num-epochs \"$NUM_EPOCHS\"\n        --learning-rate 2e-5\n        --eval-steps 500\n        --save-steps 1000\n        --use-lora\n        --gradient-checkpointing\n        --wandb-project structfuse\n        --wandb-run-name \"full_m2_bm25_s${SEED}\"\n    )\n\n    if [ \"$ESP\" -gt 0 ]; then\n        TRAIN_CMD+=(--early-stopping-patience \"$ESP\")\n    fi\n\n    echo \"CMD: ${TRAIN_CMD[*]}\" | tee \"$LOG_FILE\"\n    \"${TRAIN_CMD[@]}\" 2>&1 | tee -a \"$LOG_FILE\"\n\n    echo \"[$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')] DONE bm25_s${SEED}\"\ndone\n\necho \"\"\necho \"============================================\"\necho \" ALL BM25 QWEN RUNS COMPLETED\"\necho \" End: $(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')\"\necho \"============================================\"\nSCRIPT_EOF\nchmod +x /root/structfuse/scripts/run_m2_full_bm25_qwen.sh",
  "account": "root"
}

### 已完成

**1. 代码修改**（支持 bm25 策略）
- `training/train.py` L586: `choices` 添加 `"bm25"`
- `training/hard_negative_mining.py` `build_miner()`: 添加 `"bm25": RandomMiner`（BM25 negatives 已预挖掘在数据文件中，不需要动态挖掘）

**2. 创建的脚本**
- `/root/structfuse/scripts/run_m2_full_bm25_qwen.sh` — Qwen2.5-Coder-3B
- `/root/structfuse/scripts/run_m2_full_bm25_starcoder.sh` — StarCoder2-3B（**模型尚未下载**）

**3. 与现有脚本的差异**
- 数据路径: `train_bm25_s{seed}.jsonl` / `val_bm25_s{seed}.jsonl`
- 输出路径: `full_m2_outputs/bm25_s{seed}/`（Qwen）、`full_m2_outputs/bm25_starcoder_s{seed}/`（StarCoder）
- Epoch 配置: s42=3 epochs（无 early stopping），s43/s44=2 epochs + patience=2（现有脚本统一用 2 epochs + patience=3）
- 其他参数完全一致: batch=8, lr=2e-5, lora_r=16, proj_dim=256, temp=0.07, num_neg=7, max_seq=2048, eval_steps=500

**4. dry-run 验证**
- `bash -n` 语法检查: 两个脚本均通过
- `parse_args` 参数解析: bm25 策略正确识别
- `build_miner("bm25")`: 正常实例化为 RandomMiner
- 数据文件: 3 seeds × ~11.3K samples，路径均存在

**5. 预估单 seed 训练时间**
- 数据量与 random/embedding/dataflow 一致（~11K samples）
- s42（3 epochs）: 预估 ~2-3 小时
- s43/s44（2 epochs）: 预估 ~1.5-2 小时
- Qwen 全部 3 seeds 串行: ~5-8 小时

**6. 注意事项**
- StarCoder2-3B 模型不在服务器上（`/root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/models/` 只有 Qwen），脚本会检测并报错提示下载
- Qwen 脚本可直接启动: `nohup bash /root/structfuse/scripts/run_m2_full_bm25_qwen.sh 0 > /root/autodl-tmp/structfuse/full_m2_outputs/bm25_qwen_schedule.log 2>&1 &`
